PRIMITIVE EXECUTION API:
getp [part] <id> [qualifier] [instance] [data=[u32 or string input parameter]] [[~]act_name | [~]act_index]
                                         Execute 'GET' Primitive With Automatic
                                         Return Type and Size
  Where: act_name = Name of Action Type to Use
         act_index = Index of the Action to Use
         ~ = If Present, Indicates to Exclude that Action/Name
         (Default is to try all actions for a primitive until one succeeds)
  Note: Action Selector Options not shown in variants below, but may be used
getp_u32  [part] <id> [qualifier] [instance]        Execute U32 Get Primitive
getp_t    [part] <id> [qualifier] [instance]        Like getp But Converts Temperature
getp_part <part> <id> [qualifier] [instance]        Like getp But First Parameter must be Participant Name or ID
getp_pw   [part] <id> [qualifier] [instance]        Like getp But Converts Power To mW
getp_s    [part] <id> [qualifier] [instance]        Like getp But Return STRING
getp_b    [part] <id> [qualifier] [instance]        Like getp But Return BINARY DATA
getp_bd   [part] <id> [qualifier] [instance]        Like getp But Dumps Hex BINARY DATA
getp_bf   [part] <id> [qualifier] [instance] [file] Like getp_b But Dumps To File
getf_b    [part] <file>                             Like getp_b But Reads From File
getf_bd   [part] <file>                             Like getp_bd But Reads From File
setp [part] <id> <qualifier> <instance> <data> [[~]act_name | [~]act_index]
                                         Execute 'SET' Primitive
  Where: act_name = Name of Action Type to Use
         act_index = Index of the Action to Use
         ~ = If Present, Indicates to Exclude that Action/Name
         (Default is to try all actions for a primitive until on succeeds)
  Note: Action Selector Options not shown in variants below, but may be used
setp_t    [part] <id> <qualifier> <instance> <temp>  Execute Set as Temperature (C)
setp_part <part> <id> <qualifier> <instance> <data> [action] Like setp But First Parameter must be Participant Name or ID
setp_pw   [part] <id> <qualifier> <instance> <power> Execute Set as Power (mW)
setp_bf   [part] <id> <qualifier> <instance> <file>  Same as setp except Read from File
set_osc <id> <capablities>                  Execute ACPI _OSC Command For
                                            Listed GUIDs Below:
         Active Policy 0 = {0xd6,0x41,0xa4,0x42,0x6a,0xae,0x2b,0x46,
                            0xa8,0x4b,0x4a,0x8c,0xe7,0x90,0x27,0xd3}
         Fail Case     1 = {0xde,0xad,0xbe,0xef,0xde,0xad,0xbe,0xef,
                            0xde,0xad,0xbe,0xef,0xde,0xad,0xbe,0xef}
                                            Capabilities per APCI Spec
setb <buffer_size>                          Set Binary Buffer Size
rstp [part] <id> [qualifier] [instance]      Resets/clears an override
rstp_part <part> <id> [qualifier] [instance] Same as rstp but 1st parameter must be Participant Name or ID
getb                                        Get Binary Buffer Size
idsp [add | delete <uuid>]                  Display, add or remove a UUID from
                                            the IDSP in override.dv
CONFIG API:
config [list]                               List Open DataVaults
config files  [pattern] [...]               List Matching Data Repository Files
config open   <@datavault|repo.bin>         Open and Load a DataVault or Repository
config close  <@datavault> [...]            Close DataVault(s)
config drop   <@datavault|repo.bin> [...]   Drop Closed DataVault
config get    [@datavault] [<keyspec>] [<SHA|SHA1|-|KEYS>] [<maxkey>] [<maxdata>]
                                            Get DataVault Key (or wildcard)
config set    [@datavault] <key> <value> [type] [option ...]
                                            Set DataVault Key/Value/Type
config keys   [@datavault] [<keyspec>]      Enumerate matching Key(s) or wildcard
config delete [@datavault] <keyspec>        Delete DataVault Key (or '*')
config save   [@datavault] <key> <file>     Save a Key's binary value to a File
config exec   [@datavault] <keyspec>        Execute Script(s) in DataVault Key
config copy   [@datavault] <keyspec> [...] [@@targetdv] [option ...]
                                            Copy (and Replace) keys(s) to another DV
config merge  [@datavault] <keyspec> [...] [@@targetdv] [option ...]
                                            Merge (No Replace) keys(s) to another DV
config export {[@datavault] <keyspec> [...] ...} [@@targetdv] [asl|dv|dvx|repo] [<comment>]
                                            Export DV keys to ASL or DV File
config payload <@datavault> <file> [<class>] [compress]
                                            Manually load a file into a DataVault Payload
config rename  <oldname.ext> <newname.ext>  Rename a DataVault Repository File (Rename)
config replace <oldname.ext> <newname.ext>  Rename a DataVault Repository File (Replace)
config <copyto|appendto> <target> <file(s)> Copy or Append DataVault Repository Files to Target
config asl <encode|decode> <input> <output> Encode or Decode DataVault Repository to/from Hex ASL Text format
config doc <@datavault> <file.csv> [comment] [label] [append] Export a DV summary to file.csv
config gddv set <repo.bin|@@factory>        Set a GDDV override or do a Factory Reset and Restart Apps
config gddv reset                           Clear a GDDV override and Restart Apps
config gddv backup                          Backup GDDV-related DV Files without Restarting Apps
config gddv restore                         Restore GDDV-related DV Files from Backup and Restart Apps
config gddv mode                            Display Current GDDV Emulation Mode (Normal/Emulation)
config gddv status                          Display Current GDDV Modified Status (Unchanged/Modified)
config rekey [@datavault]                   Rekey IETM Participant Keys in GDDV Exports
config comment <@datavault|repo.bin> [<comment>] Get or Set DataVault or Repo Comment
config version [@datavault] <major.minor>   Set DataVault File Version
config upload [overwrite|append] <filename> <base64-data> Upload a file from a base64-encoded string

USER-MODE PARTICIPANT DATA LOGGING:
participantlog start [all |[PID DID capMask]...]
                                        Starts data logging for that particular
                                        participant ID,domain and capability ID
                                        combination
                                        all     - Starts data logging for all
                                                  the available participants
                                        PID     - Participant ID (e.g 1,2)|
                                                  Participant Name(e.g TCHG,
                                                  TCPU)
                                        DID     - Domain ID(e.g D0,D1)|all
                                                  (for all domains)
                                        capMask - capability Mask(e.g 0x2100)|
                                                  all(for all capabilities)
                                        If no arguments are specified for start
                                        command, by default logging will start
                                        for all the available participants
                                        Capability Mask Details:
                                        0x%08X -  %s
participantlog schedule [delay] [all |[PID DID capMask]...]
                                        Start a delayed logging thread ms
                                        from now.
                                        delay - Time interval in ms. (Default 
                                        5000ms)
                                        Other arguments list descriptions are
                                        same as the start command.
participantlog interval [time]          Sets the polling interval for logging
                                        to the time specified in ms.
                                        time - Polling interval in ms. Supported
                                        values: 250 to 65535ms (Default 1000ms)
participantlog route [ALL | [target ... [filename]]] 
                                        Logs the participant data log to the
                                        specified target.
                                        If no arguments are specified, data is
                                        routed to a file.*
                                        If ALL is specified, data is sent to
                                        all available targets.
                                        The target can be any of the following:
                                        CONSOLE, EVENTVIEWER, DEBUGGER, or FILE.
                                        If FILE is specified as the target, the
                                        next argument, if present, must specify
                                        the file name.*
                                        *If a filename is not specified, a default
                                        file name is used based on the timestamp;
                                        e.g, participant_log_2015-11-24-142412.csv.
participantlog stop                     Stops participant data logging if
                                        started already
